-- Proxy ms
local __alphabet={"1","0","%","A","!","$","n","g","x","&","d","2","#","c","@","l"};local __lookup={};for i=1,#__alphabet do __lookup[__alphabet[i]]=i-1 end;local function __decode(data,key)local out={};local high=nil;local i=1;while i<=#data do local nibble=__lookup[data:sub(i,i)];if nibble==nil then error('invalid packed payload symbol') end;if high==nil then high=nibble else out[#out+1]=string.char((high*16+nibble-key)%256);high=nil end;i=i+1 end;if high~=nil then error('invalid packed payload length') end;return table.concat(out) end;local __loader=loadstring or load;if type(__loader)~="function" then error("StringToExpressions requires loadstring; this environment cannot execute decoded scripts") end;local
